Definition 3.8
.

[
33
]

A post-Lie algebra is a triple
(\mathfrak{g},\{-,-\},\triangleleft)
where
\mathfrak{g}
is a vector space and
\{-,-\},\triangleleft
are bilinear products on
\mathfrak{g}
such that

\displaystyle\forall x,y,z\in\mathfrak{g},

\displaystyle\{\{x,y\},z\}+\{\{y,z\},x\}+\{\{z,x\},y\}=0.

(2)

\displaystyle x\triangleleft\{y,z\}=\{x\triangleleft y,z\}+\{y,x\triangleleft z\}.

(3)

\displaystyle\{x,y\}\triangleleft z=x\triangleleft(y\triangleleft z)-(x\triangleleft y)\triangleleft z-y\triangleleft(x\triangleleft z)+(y\triangleleft x)\triangleleft z.

(4)

In particular, pre-Lie algebras are post-Lie algebras with a zero bracket
\{-,-\}
.

We now assume that
(P,\{-,-\},\triangleleft)
is a post-Lie algebra.
Our aim in this section is to extend this post-Lie structure and the pre-Lie structure of
E\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
to a post-Lie algebra structure on
\left(E\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})\right)\oplus P
.

Proposition 3.9
.

Let
P
be a post-Lie algebra and let two maps

\displaystyle\psi_{V}

\displaystyle:\left\{\begin{array}[]{rcl}P&\longrightarrow&\mathrm{End}(D_{V})\\
p&\longmapsto&\left\{\begin{array}[]{rcl}D_{V}&\longrightarrow&D_{V}\\
b&\longmapsto&\psi_{V}(p)(b),\end{array}\right.\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\psi_{E}

\displaystyle:\left\{\begin{array}[]{rcl}P&\longrightarrow&\mathrm{End}(D_{E})\\
p&\longmapsto&\left\{\begin{array}[]{rcl}D_{V}&\longrightarrow&D_{V}\\
a&\longmapsto&\psi_{E}(p)(a).\end{array}\right.\end{array}\right.

We extend the structure maps of
P
as a bilinear map
\triangleleft
and an antisymmetric bilinear map
\{-,-\}
on
\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{P}(D_{E},D_{V})=\left(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})\right)\oplus P
by the following: for any
p,p^{\prime}\in P
, for any
a,a^{\prime}\in D_{E}
and for any

x=T\otimes W_{T}
and
x^{\prime}=T^{\prime}\otimes W_{T^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
,we put

\displaystyle a\otimes x\triangleleft a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\sum_{v_{0}\in V(T^{\prime})}a^{\prime}\otimes T\curvearrowright_{v_{0}}T^{\prime}\otimes\phi_{1,v_{0}}\left(a\otimes W_{T}\otimes W_{T^{\prime}}\right),

\displaystyle p\triangleleft a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\sum_{v_{0}\in V(T^{\prime})}a^{\prime}\otimes T^{\prime}\otimes\psi_{V}(p)_{v_{0}}(W_{T^{\prime}}),

\displaystyle a\otimes x\triangleleft p^{\prime}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\{a\otimes x,a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime}\}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\{a\otimes x,p^{\prime}\}

\displaystyle=\psi_{E}(p^{\prime})(a)\otimes x.

Then
(\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{P}(D_{E},D_{V}),\triangleleft,\{-,-\})
is a post-Lie algebra if, and only if,

\displaystyle\forall p,p^{\prime}\in P,

\displaystyle\psi_{E}(\{p,p^{\prime}\})

\displaystyle=\psi_{E}(p^{\prime})\circ\psi_{E}(p)-\psi_{E}(p)\circ\psi_{E}(p^{\prime}),

(5)

\displaystyle\psi_{E}(p\triangleleft p^{\prime})

\displaystyle=0,

(6)

\displaystyle\psi_{V}(\{p,p^{\prime}\})

\displaystyle=\psi_{V}(p)\circ\psi_{V}(p^{\prime})-\psi_{V}(p^{\prime})\circ\psi_{V}(p)

\displaystyle-\psi_{V}(p\triangleleft p^{\prime})+\psi_{V}(p^{\prime}\triangleleft p),

(7)

\displaystyle\forall p\in P,

\displaystyle\phi\circ(\psi_{E}(p)\otimes\mathrm{Id}_{D_{V}})

\displaystyle=\phi\circ(\mathrm{Id}_{D_{E}}\otimes\psi_{V}(p))-(\mathrm{Id}_{D_{E}}\otimes\psi_{V}(p))\circ\phi.

(8)

Proof.

Jacobi relation (
2
) for
\{-,-\}
. Observe that
(P,\{-,-\})
is a Lie algebra and
D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
is an abelian Lie algebra. Therefore, to obtain the Jacobi relation, it is enough to consider two cases.

1.

First case:
x,y\in D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
and
z\in P
. With immediate notations,

\displaystyle\{\{a\otimes x,a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime}\},p^{\prime\prime}\}+\{\hskip-5.69054pt\underbrace{\{a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime},p^{\prime\prime}\}}_{\in D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})}\hskip-5.69054pt,a\otimes x\}+\{\hskip-8.53581pt\underbrace{\{p^{\prime\prime},a\otimes x\}}_{\in D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})}\hskip-8.53581pt,a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime}\}=0+0+0=0.

2.

Second case:
x\in D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
,
y,z\in P
. With immediate notations,

\displaystyle\{\{a\otimes x,p^{\prime}\},p^{\prime\prime}\}+\{\{p^{\prime},p^{\prime\prime}\},a\otimes x\}+\{\{p^{\prime\prime},a\otimes x\},p^{\prime}\}

\displaystyle=\{\psi_{E}(p^{\prime})(a)\otimes x,p^{\prime\prime}\}-\psi_{E}(\{p^{\prime},p^{\prime\prime}\})(a)\otimes x-\{\psi_{E}(p^{\prime\prime})(a)\otimes x,p^{\prime}\}

\displaystyle=\psi_{E}(p^{\prime\prime})\circ\psi_{E}(p^{\prime})(a)\otimes x-\psi_{E}(\{p^{\prime},p^{\prime\prime}\})(a)\otimes x-\psi_{E}(p^{\prime})\circ\psi_{E}(p^{\prime\prime})(a)\otimes x.

Therefore, (
2
) is satisfied if, and only if, (
5
) is satisfied.

First post-Lie relation (
3
)
. By hypothesis, it is satisfied on the post-Lie algebra
P
.
As the bracket is zero on
D_{E}
and on
D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
, and using its anti-symmetry, we can restrict ourselves to four cases.

1.

x,y\in D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
,
z\in P
. Then

\displaystyle a\otimes x\triangleleft\{a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime},p^{\prime\prime}\}

\displaystyle=a\otimes x\triangleleft\psi_{E}(p^{\prime\prime})(a^{\prime})\otimes x^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\psi_{E}(p^{\prime\prime})(a^{\prime})\otimes x\triangleleft_{a}^{\phi}x^{\prime},

\displaystyle\{a\otimes x\triangleleft a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime},p^{\prime\prime}\}+\{a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime},a\otimes x\triangleleft p^{\prime\prime}\}

\displaystyle=\{a^{\prime}\otimes x\triangleleft_{a}^{\phi}x^{\prime},p^{\prime\prime}\}+0

\displaystyle=\psi_{E}(p^{\prime\prime})(a^{\prime})\otimes x\triangleleft_{a}^{\phi}x^{\prime}.

2.

x\in P
,
y,z\in D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
. Then

\displaystyle p\triangleleft\{a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime},a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime}\}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\{\hskip-7.11317pt\underbrace{p\triangleleft a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime}}_{\in D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})}\hskip-7.11317pt,a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime}\}+\{a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime},\hskip-5.69054pt\underbrace{p\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime}}_{\in D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})}\hskip-5.69054pt\}

\displaystyle=0+0=0.

3.

x\in D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
,
y,z\in P
. Then

\displaystyle a\otimes x\triangleleft\{p^{\prime},p^{\prime\prime}\}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\{a\otimes x\triangleleft p^{\prime},p^{\prime\prime}\}+\{p^{\prime},a\otimes x\triangleleft p^{\prime\prime}\}

\displaystyle=0+0=0.

4.

x,z\in P
,
y\in D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
. Then

\displaystyle p\triangleleft\{a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime},p^{\prime\prime}\}

\displaystyle=p\triangleleft\psi_{E}(p^{\prime\prime})(a^{\prime})\otimes x^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\sum_{v_{0}\in V(T^{\prime})}\psi_{E}(p^{\prime\prime})(a^{\prime})\otimes\psi_{V}(p)_{v_{0}}(x),

\displaystyle\{p\triangleleft a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime},p^{\prime\prime}\}+\{a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime},p\triangleleft p^{\prime\prime}\}

\displaystyle=\sum_{v_{0}\in V(T^{\prime})}\{a^{\prime}\otimes\psi_{V}(p)_{v_{0}}(x^{\prime}),v^{\prime\prime}\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{v_{0}\in V(T^{\prime})}\psi_{E}(p\triangleleft p^{\prime\prime})(a^{\prime})\otimes x^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\sum_{v_{0}\in V(T^{\prime})}\psi_{E}(p^{\prime\prime})(a^{\prime})\otimes\psi_{V}(p)_{v_{0}}(x)

\displaystyle+\sum_{v_{0}\in V(T^{\prime})}\psi_{E}(p\triangleleft p^{\prime\prime})(a^{\prime})\otimes x^{\prime}.

So (
3
) is satisfied if, and only if (
6
) is satisfied.

Second post-Lie relation (
4
)
. We already notice that
(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V}),\triangleleft)
is pre-Lie. As the bracket is zero on this subspace, the second post-Lie relation is satisfied on
D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
.
It is also satisfied on the post-Lie algebra
P
. Using the anti-symmetry on
a,b
, we can restrict ourselves to four cases.

1.

x,y\in D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
and
z\in P
. Then

\displaystyle\{a\otimes x,a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime}\}\triangleleft p^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle a\otimes x\triangleleft(a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime}\triangleleft p^{\prime\prime})-(\underbrace{a\otimes x\triangleleft a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime}}_{\in D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})})\triangleleft p^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=0+0=0.

2.

y,z\in P
,
x\in D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
. Then

\displaystyle\underbrace{\{a\triangleleft x,p^{\prime}\}}_{\in D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})}\triangleleft p^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=0,

whereas

\displaystyle a\otimes x\triangleleft(p^{\prime}\otimes p^{\prime\prime})-(a\otimes x\triangleleft p^{\prime})\triangleleft p^{\prime\prime}-p^{\prime}\triangleleft(a\otimes x\triangleleft p^{\prime\prime})+(\hskip-8.53581pt\underbrace{p^{\prime}\triangleleft a\otimes x}_{\in D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})}\hskip-8.53581pt)\triangleleft p^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=0+0+0+0=0.

3.

x,y\in P
,
z\in D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
. Then

\displaystyle\{p,p^{\prime}\}\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=\sum_{v_{0}\in V(T^{\prime\prime})}a^{\prime\prime}\otimes\psi_{V}(\{p^{\prime},p^{\prime\prime}\})_{v_{0}}(x^{\prime\prime}),

whereas

\displaystyle p\triangleleft(p^{\prime}\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime})-(p\triangleleft p^{\prime})\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime}-p^{\prime}\triangleleft(p\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime})+(p^{\prime}\triangleleft p)\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=\sum_{v_{0},v_{1}\in V(T^{\prime\prime})}a^{\prime\prime}\otimes\psi_{V}(p)_{v_{1}}\circ\psi_{V}(p^{\prime})_{v_{0}}(x^{\prime\prime})-\sum_{v_{0}\in V(T^{\prime\prime})}a^{\prime\prime}\otimes\psi_{V}(p\triangleleft p^{\prime})_{v_{0}}(x^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle-\sum_{v_{0},v_{1}\in V(T^{\prime\prime})}a^{\prime\prime}\otimes\psi_{V}(p^{\prime})_{v_{0}}\circ\psi_{V}(p)_{v_{1}}(x^{\prime\prime})+\sum_{v_{0}\in V(T^{\prime\prime})}a^{\prime\prime}\otimes\psi_{V}(p^{\prime}\triangleleft p^{\prime\prime})_{v_{0}}(x^{\prime\prime}).

If
v_{0}\neq v_{1}
, obviously
\psi_{V}(p)_{v_{0}}
and
\psi_{V}(p)_{v_{1}}
commute. Therefore,

\displaystyle p\triangleleft(p^{\prime}\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime})-(p\triangleleft p^{\prime})\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime}-p^{\prime}\triangleleft(p\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime})+(p^{\prime}\triangleleft p)\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=\sum_{v_{0}\in V(T^{\prime\prime})}a^{\prime\prime}\otimes\psi_{V}(p)_{v_{0}}\circ\psi_{V}(p^{\prime})_{v_{0}}(x^{\prime\prime})-\sum_{v_{0}\in V(T^{\prime\prime})}a^{\prime\prime}\otimes\psi_{V}(p\triangleleft p^{\prime})_{v_{0}}(x^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle-\sum_{v_{0}\in V(T^{\prime\prime})}a^{\prime\prime}\otimes\psi_{V}(p^{\prime})_{v_{0}}\circ\psi_{V}(p)_{v_{0}}(x^{\prime\prime})+\sum_{v_{0}\in V(T^{\prime\prime})}a^{\prime\prime}\otimes\psi_{V}(p^{\prime}\triangleleft p^{\prime\prime})_{v_{0}}(x^{\prime\prime}).

Hence, in this third case, (
4
) is equivalent to (
7
).

4.

x,z\in D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
,
y\in P
. Then, with immediate notations,

\displaystyle\{a\otimes x,p^{\prime}\}\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=\psi_{E}(p^{\prime})(a)\otimes x\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x\triangleleft_{\psi_{E}(p^{\prime})(a)}x^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=\sum_{v_{0}\in V(T^{\prime\prime})}a^{\prime\prime}\otimes T\curvearrowright_{v_{0}}T^{\prime\prime}\otimes\phi_{1,v_{0}}\circ\psi_{E}(p^{\prime})_{0}(a\otimes W_{T}\otimes W_{T^{\prime\prime}}).

On the other side,

\displaystyle a\otimes x\triangleleft(p^{\prime}\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle=\sum_{v_{0}\in V(T^{\prime\prime})}a\otimes x\triangleleft p^{\prime\prime}\otimes T^{\prime\prime}\otimes\psi_{V}(p^{\prime})_{v_{0}}\left(W_{T^{\prime\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{v_{0},v_{1}\in V(T^{\prime\prime})}a^{\prime\prime}\otimes T\curvearrowright_{v_{1}}T^{\prime\prime}\otimes\phi_{1,v_{1}}\circ\psi_{V}(a^{\prime})_{v_{0}}(a\otimes W_{T}\otimes W_{T^{\prime\prime}}),

\displaystyle(a\otimes x\triangleleft p^{\prime})\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle p^{\prime}\triangleleft(a\otimes x\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle=\sum_{v_{1}\in V(T^{\prime\prime})}p^{\prime}\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes T\curvearrowright_{v_{1}}T^{\prime\prime}\otimes\phi_{1,v_{1}}(a\otimes W_{T}\otimes W_{T^{\prime\prime}})

\displaystyle=\sum_{v_{0},v_{1}\in V(T^{\prime\prime})}a^{\prime\prime}\otimes T\curvearrowright_{v_{1}}T^{\prime\prime}\otimes\psi_{V}(p^{\prime})_{v_{0}}\circ\phi_{1,v_{1}})

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v_{0}\in V(T),\\
v_{1}\in V(T^{\prime\prime})\end{subarray}}a^{\prime\prime}\otimes T\curvearrowright_{v_{1}}T^{\prime\prime}\otimes\psi_{V}(p^{\prime})_{v_{0}}\circ\phi_{1,v_{1}}(a\otimes W_{T}\otimes W_{T^{\prime\prime}}),

\displaystyle(p^{\prime}\triangleleft a\otimes x)\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=\sum_{v_{0}\in V(T)}a\otimes T\otimes\psi_{V}(p^{\prime})_{v_{0}}\left(W_{T}\right)\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v_{0}\in V(T),\\
v_{1}\in V(T^{\prime\prime})\end{subarray}}a^{\prime\prime}\otimes T\curvearrowright_{v_{1}}T^{\prime\prime}\otimes\phi_{1,v_{1}}\circ\psi_{V}(p^{\prime})_{v_{0}}(a\otimes W_{T}\otimes W_{T^{\prime\prime}}).

If
v_{0}\in V(T)
and
v_{1}\in V(T^{\prime\prime})
or if
v_{0},v_{1}
are two different vertices of
T^{\prime\prime}
, obviously
\phi_{1,v_{1}}
and
\psi_{V}(p^{\prime})_{v_{0}}
commute. Therefore, we obtain finally

\displaystyle a\otimes x\triangleleft(p^{\prime}\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime})-(a\otimes x\triangleleft p^{\prime})\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime}-p^{\prime}\triangleleft(a\otimes x\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime})+(p^{\prime}\triangleleft a\otimes x)\triangleleft a^{\prime\prime}\otimes x^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=\sum_{v_{0}\in V(T^{\prime\prime})}a^{\prime\prime}\otimes T\curvearrowright_{v_{0}}T^{\prime\prime}\otimes\phi_{2,v_{0}}\circ\psi_{V}(p^{\prime})_{v_{0}}(a\otimes W_{T}\otimes W_{T^{\prime\prime}}).

As a consequence, we obtain that (
4
) is satisfied in this last case if and only if, for any
v_{0}\in V(T^{\prime\prime})
,

\phi_{1,v_{0}}\circ\psi_{E}(p^{\prime})_{0}=\phi_{1,v_{0}}\circ\psi_{V}(p^{\prime}_{v_{0}})-\psi_{V}(p^{\prime})_{v_{0}}\circ\phi_{1,v_{0}},

which is equivalent to (
8
). ∎

Example 3.4
.

If
p\in P
and
a_{1},a_{2},a_{3}\in D_{E}
,
b_{1},b_{2},b_{3}\in D_{V}
,

\displaystyle p\triangleleft\hskip-14.22636pt\begin{array}[]{c}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{2}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern \displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{c}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 7.38889pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\hbox{\vtop{\halign{\entry@#!@&&\entry@@#!@\cr&\cr&\cr&\crcr}}}\ignorespaces{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-7.38889pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{2}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 15.26865pt\raise-15.6111pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a_{2}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern 31.38889pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{3}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 34.65753pt\raise-15.6111pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a_{3}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\kern-3.0pt\raise-31.2222pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.5pt\hbox{$\textstyle{}$}}}}}}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern 31.38889pt\raise-31.2222pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{1}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\kern-3.0pt\raise-61.49649pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.5pt\hbox{$\textstyle{}$}}}}}}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern 33.27777pt\raise-61.49649pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.3264pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\star}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 34.65753pt\raise-46.35934pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a_{1}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\end{array},p\right\}

\displaystyle=\begin{array}[]{c}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 7.38889pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\hbox{\vtop{\halign{\entry@#!@&&\entry@@#!@\cr&\cr&\cr&\crcr}}}\ignorespaces{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-7.38889pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{2}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 15.26865pt\raise-15.6111pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a_{2}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern 31.38889pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{3}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 34.65753pt\raise-15.6111pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a_{3}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\kern-3.0pt\raise-31.2222pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.5pt\hbox{$\textstyle{}$}}}}}}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern 31.38889pt\raise-31.2222pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{1}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\kern-3.0pt\raise-61.49649pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.5pt\hbox{$\textstyle{}$}}}}}}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern 33.27777pt\raise-61.49649pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.3264pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\star}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 20.80461pt\raise-46.35934pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.75pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\psi_{E}(p)(a_{1})}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\end{array}.

Remark 3.1
.

1.

Relation (
5
) means that
-\psi_{E}:(P,\{-,-\})\longrightarrow(\mathrm{End}(D_{E}),[-,-])
is a Lie algebra morphism, where
[-,-]
is the usual bracket of endomorphisms.

2.

The post-Lie algebra
P
has a second Lie bracket, defined by

\displaystyle\forall p,p^{\prime}\in P,

\displaystyle\{\{p,p^{\prime}\}\}=\{p,p^{\prime}\}+p\triangleleft p^{\prime}-p^{\prime}\triangleleft p.

Relation (
7
) means that
\psi_{V}:(P,\{\{-,-\}\})\longrightarrow(\mathrm{End}(D_{V}),[-,-])
is a Lie algebra morphism.

In the case where
P
is trivial, this simplifies:

Corollary 3.10
.

Let
P
be a vector space and two maps

\displaystyle\psi_{V}

\displaystyle:\left\{\begin{array}[]{rcl}P&\longrightarrow&\mathrm{End}(D_{V})\\
p&\longmapsto&\left\{\begin{array}[]{rcl}D_{V}&\longrightarrow&D_{V}\\
b&\longmapsto&\psi_{V}(p)(b),\end{array}\right.\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\psi_{E}

\displaystyle:\left\{\begin{array}[]{rcl}P&\longrightarrow&\mathrm{End}(D_{E})\\
p&\longmapsto&\left\{\begin{array}[]{rcl}D_{V}&\longrightarrow&D_{V}\\
a&\longmapsto&\psi_{E}(p)(a).\end{array}\right.\end{array}\right.

We define a bilinear map
\triangleleft
and an antisymmetric bilinear map
\{-,-\}
on
\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{P}(D_{E},D_{V})=\left(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})\right)\oplus D_{E}
by the following: for any
p,p^{\prime}\in P
and for any
x=T\otimes W_{T}
and
x^{\prime}=T^{\prime}\otimes W_{T^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
, we put

\displaystyle a\otimes x\triangleleft a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\sum_{v_{0}\in V(T^{\prime})}a^{\prime}\otimes T\curvearrowright_{v_{0}}T^{\prime}\otimes\phi_{1,v_{0}}\left(a\otimes W_{T}\otimes W_{T^{\prime}}\right),

\displaystyle p\triangleleft a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\sum_{v_{0}\in V(T^{\prime})}a^{\prime}\otimes T^{\prime}\otimes\psi_{V}(p)(a^{\prime})_{v_{0}}(W_{T^{\prime}}),

\displaystyle a\otimes x\triangleleft p^{\prime}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle p\triangleleft p^{\prime}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\{a\otimes x,a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime}\}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\{a\otimes x,p^{\prime}\}

\displaystyle=\psi_{E}(p^{\prime})(a)\otimes x,

\displaystyle\{p,p^{\prime}\}

\displaystyle=0.

Then
(\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{P}(D_{E},D_{V}),\triangleleft,\{-,-\})
is a post-Lie algebra if, and only if,

\displaystyle\forall p,p^{\prime}\in P,

\displaystyle\psi_{E}(p^{\prime})\circ\psi_{E}(p)

\displaystyle=\psi_{E}(p)\circ\psi_{E}(p^{\prime}),

(9)

\displaystyle\forall p,p^{\prime}\in P,

\displaystyle\psi_{V}(p)\circ\psi_{V}(p^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\psi_{V}(p^{\prime})\circ\psi_{V}(p),

(10)

\displaystyle\forall p\in P,

\displaystyle\phi\circ(\psi_{E}(p)\otimes\mathrm{Id}_{D_{V}})

\displaystyle=\phi\circ(\mathrm{Id}_{D_{E}}\otimes\psi_{V}(p))-(\mathrm{Id}_{D_{E}}\otimes\psi_{V}(p))\circ\phi.

(11)

If
(\psi_{E},\psi_{V})
satisfies these three conditions, we shall say that
(\psi_{E},\psi_{V})
is
\phi
-compatible.

3.3
Guin-Oudom construction

We now apply the Guin-Oudom construction
[
30
,
31
]
to the pre-Lie algebra
(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V}),\triangleleft^{\phi})
, when
\phi
is tree-compatible.
The extension of
\triangleleft^{\phi}
to
S((D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V}))
is also denoted by
\triangleleft^{\phi}
and the associated product by
\star^{\phi}
. Then
S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V}))
, with the product
\star^{\phi}
and its usual coproduct
is a Hopf algebra, isomorphic to the enveloping algebra of the Lie algebra underlying the pre-Lie algebra
(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V}),\triangleleft^{\phi})

Proposition 3.11
.

1.

Let
a_{1}\otimes x_{1},\ldots,a_{k}\otimes x_{k},a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime}\in D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
. Then

\displaystyle a_{1}\otimes x_{1}\cdots a_{k}\otimes x_{k}\triangleleft^{\phi}a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\sum_{v_{1},\ldots,v_{k}\in V(T^{\prime})}a^{\prime}\otimes T_{1}\curvearrowright_{v_{1}}(\cdots(T_{k}\curvearrowright_{v_{k}}T^{\prime})\cdots)

\displaystyle\otimes\phi_{1,v_{1}}\circ\cdots\circ\phi_{k,v_{k}}(a_{1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{k}\otimes W_{T_{1}}\otimes\cdots\otimes W_{T_{k}}\otimes W_{T^{\prime}}).

2.

Let
a_{1}\otimes x_{1},\ldots,a_{k}\otimes x_{k},a^{\prime}_{1}\otimes x^{\prime}_{1},\ldots,a^{\prime}_{l}\otimes x^{\prime}_{l}\in D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
. Then

\displaystyle a_{1}\otimes x_{1}\cdots a_{k}\otimes x_{k}\triangleleft^{\phi}a^{\prime}_{1}\otimes x^{\prime}_{1}\cdots\otimes a^{\prime}_{l}\otimes x^{\prime}_{l}

\displaystyle=\sum_{[k]=I_{1}\sqcup\cdots\sqcup I_{l}}\prod_{j=1}^{l}\left(\left(\prod_{i\in I_{j}}a_{i}\otimes x_{i}\right)\triangleleft^{\phi}a^{\prime}_{j}\otimes x^{\prime}_{j}\right).

3.

Let
a_{1}\otimes x_{1},\ldots,a_{k}\otimes x_{k},a^{\prime}_{1}\otimes x^{\prime}_{1},\ldots,a^{\prime}_{l}\otimes x^{\prime}_{l}\in D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
. Then

\displaystyle a_{1}\otimes x_{1}\cdots a_{k}\otimes x_{k}\star^{\phi}a^{\prime}_{1}\otimes x^{\prime}_{1}\cdots\otimes a^{\prime}_{l}\otimes x^{\prime}_{l}

\displaystyle=\sum_{[k]=I_{0}\sqcup I_{1}\sqcup\cdots\sqcup I_{l}}\left(\prod_{i\in I_{0}}a_{i}\otimes x_{i}\right)\prod_{j=1}^{l}\left(\left(\prod_{i\in I_{j}}a_{i}\otimes x_{i}\right)\triangleleft^{\phi}a^{\prime}_{j}\otimes x^{\prime}_{j}\right).

Proof.

The second and third points are direct applications of the Guin-Oudom construction. Let us prove the first point by induction on
k
. If
k=1
, this is immediately implied by the definition of
\triangleleft^{\phi}
by graftings.
Let us assume the result at rank
k-1
. In order to lighten the writing, we put

\displaystyle W

\displaystyle=a_{1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{k}\otimes W_{T_{1}}\otimes\cdots\otimes W_{T_{k}}\otimes W_{T^{\prime}},

\displaystyle W^{\prime}

\displaystyle=a_{2}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{k}\otimes W_{T_{2}}\otimes\cdots\otimes W_{T_{k}}\otimes W_{T^{\prime}}.

Then

\displaystyle a_{1}\otimes x_{1}\cdots a_{k}\otimes x_{k}\triangleleft^{\phi}a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime}

\displaystyle=a_{1}\otimes x_{1}\triangleleft^{\phi}\left(\sum_{v_{2},\ldots,v_{k}\in V(T^{\prime})}a^{\prime}\otimes T_{2}\curvearrowright_{v_{2}}(\cdots(T_{k}\curvearrowright_{v_{k}}T^{\prime})\cdots)\otimes\phi_{2,v_{2}}\circ\cdots\circ\phi_{k,v_{k}}(W^{\prime})\right)

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=2}^{k}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v_{2},\ldots,v_{k}\in V(T^{\prime}),\\
v_{1}\in V(T_{i})\end{subarray}}a^{\prime}\otimes T_{2}\curvearrowright_{v_{2}}(\cdots(T_{1}\curvearrowright_{v_{1}}T_{i})\curvearrowright_{v_{i}}(\cdots(T_{k}\curvearrowright_{v_{k}}T^{\prime}))\cdots)

\displaystyle\otimes\phi_{2,v_{2}}\circ\cdots\circ\phi_{k,v_{k}}\circ\phi_{1,v_{1}}(W)

\displaystyle=\sum_{v_{1},\ldots,v_{k}\in V(T^{\prime})}a^{\prime}\otimes T_{1}\curvearrowright_{v_{1}}(\cdots(T_{k}\curvearrowright_{v_{k}}T^{\prime})\cdots)\otimes\phi_{1,v_{1}}\circ\cdots\circ\phi_{k,v_{k}}(W)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=2}^{k}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v_{2},\ldots,v_{k}\in V(T^{\prime}),\\
v_{1}\in V(T_{i})\end{subarray}}a^{\prime}\otimes T_{1}\curvearrowright_{v_{1}}(\cdots(T_{k}\curvearrowright_{v_{k}}T^{\prime})\cdots)\otimes\phi_{1,v_{1}}\circ\cdots\circ\phi_{k,v_{k}}(W)

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=2}^{k}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v_{2},\ldots,v_{k}\in V(T^{\prime}),\\
v_{1}\in V(T_{i})\end{subarray}}a^{\prime}\otimes T_{2}\curvearrowright_{v_{2}}(\cdots(T_{1}\curvearrowright_{v_{1}}T_{i})\curvearrowright_{v_{i}}(\cdots(T_{k}\curvearrowright_{v_{k}}T^{\prime}))\cdots)

\displaystyle\otimes\phi_{2,v_{2}}\circ\cdots\circ\phi_{k,v_{k}}\circ\phi_{1,v_{1}}(W).

We conclude with the equality of the trees

T_{2}\curvearrowright_{v_{2}}(\cdots(T_{1}\curvearrowright_{v_{1}}T_{i})\curvearrowright_{v_{i}}(\cdots(T_{k}\curvearrowright_{v_{k}}T^{\prime}))=T_{1}\curvearrowright_{v_{1}}(\cdots(T_{k}\curvearrowright_{v_{k}}T^{\prime})\cdots)

when
v_{2},\ldots,v_{k}\in V(T^{\prime})
and
v_{1}\in V(T_{i})
with
i\geq 2
and by the commutation of the
\phi_{i,v_{i}}
by the tree-compatibility of
\phi
. It remains

\displaystyle a_{1}\otimes x_{1}\cdots a_{k}\otimes x_{k}\triangleleft^{\phi}a^{\prime}\otimes x^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\sum_{v_{1},\ldots,v_{k}\in V(T^{\prime})}a^{\prime}\otimes T_{1}\curvearrowright_{v_{1}}(\cdots(T_{k}\curvearrowright_{v_{k}}T^{\prime})\cdots)\otimes\phi_{1,v_{1}}\circ\cdots\circ\phi_{k,v_{k}}(W).\qed

.

3.0pt\raise-2.3264pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\star}$}}}}}\ignorespaces}}}}\end{array}.

Let us give a combinatorial description of
\star^{\phi}
. We shall need the following notion:

Definition 3.12
.

Let
F
and
G
be two forests of planted rooted trees. The set of trees of
F
is denoted by
\mathbf{T}(F)
. A grafting of
F
over
G
is a map
g:\mathbf{T}(F)\longrightarrow V(G)\sqcup\{0\}
.
The set of graftings of
F
over
G
is denoted by
\mathbf{G}(F,G)
. If
g\in\mathbf{G}(F,G)
, then
F\curvearrowright_{g}G
is the forest of planted rooted trees obtained from
FG
by identifying the undecorated root of
T
with
g(T)
for any
T\in\mathbf{T}(F)
such that
g(T)\neq 0
.

Then:

Proposition 3.13
.

Let
x=F\otimes W_{F}
and
y=G\otimes W_{G}
in
S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E}\otimes D_{V}))
. Then

x\star^{\phi}y=\sum_{g\in\mathbf{G}(F,G)}F\curvearrowright_{g}G\otimes\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}\in\mathbf{T}(F),\\
g(T)\neq 0\end{subarray}}\phi_{e_{T},g(T)}(W_{F}\otimes W_{G}),

where
e_{T}
is the decoration of the unique edge whose source is the undecorated root of
T
.

We extend
\Theta_{\phi}
to
D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
by
\mathrm{Id}_{D_{E}}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})
, which in turn is extended to
S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V}))
as an algebra morphism. This extension is denoted by
\overline{\Theta}_{\phi}
.

Example 3.6
.

Let
a_{1},a_{2},a_{3}\in D_{E}
and
b_{1},b_{2},b_{3}\in D_{E}
.

\displaystyle\overline{\Theta}_{\phi}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 7.38889pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\hbox{\vtop{\halign{\entry@#!@&&\entry@@#!@\cr&\cr&\cr&\crcr}}}\ignorespaces{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-7.38889pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{1}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 15.26865pt\raise-15.6111pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{3}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 34.65753pt\raise-46.35934pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern \displaystyle=\sum_{i,j}\begin{array}[]{c}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 17.045pt\raise-15.90065pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 51.7917pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{2}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 46.6353pt\raise-15.90065pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.30008pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\phi_{E}^{j}(a_{2})}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\kern-3.0pt\raise-31.8013pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.5pt\hbox{$\textstyle{}$}}}}}}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern 31.38889pt\raise-31.8013pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.88043pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\phi_{V}^{i}\circ\phi_{V}^{j}(b_{3})}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 55.06035pt\raise-47.228pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a_{3}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\kern-3.0pt\raise-62.6547pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.5pt\hbox{$\textstyle{}$}}}}}}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern 53.68059pt\raise-62.6547pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.3264pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\star}$}}}}}\ignorespaces}}}}\end{array}.

The functoriality of the Guin-Oudom construction gives:

Proposition 3.14
.

Let
\phi,\psi
be two tree-compatible maps on
D_{E}\otimes D_{V}
such that

\psi_{13}\circ\phi_{23}=\phi_{23}\circ\psi_{13}.

The map
\overline{\Theta}_{\phi}
is a morphism from the bialgebra
(S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})),\star^{\psi},\Delta)
to the bialgebra
(S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})),\star^{\phi\circ\psi},\Delta)
.
It is an isomorphism if, and only if,
\phi
is bijective.

A particular case is
\phi=\mathrm{Id}_{D_{E}\otimes D_{V}}
. In this case, we simply write
\star
instead of
\star^{\mathrm{Id}_{D_{E}\otimes D_{V}}}
. We obtain:

Corollary 3.15
.

Let
\phi
be a tree-compatible map
D_{E}\otimes D_{V}
.
The map
\overline{\Theta}_{\phi}
is a bialgebra morphism from
(S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})),\star,\Delta)
to

(S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})),\star^{\phi},\Delta)
.
It is an isomorphism if, and only if,
\phi
is bijective.

3.4
Butcher-Connes-Kreimer construction

We here define a Butcher-Connes-Kreimer-like bialgebra structure associated to a tree-compatible map
\phi
.
We shall need the following combinatorial notions:

Definition 3.16
.

Let
F
be a forest of planted rooted trees. Its set of vertices (which do not include the roots decorated by
\star
) is denoted by
V(F)
and its set of edges by
E(F)
.

1.

An upper part of
F
is a subset
I
of
V(F)
such that
for any
x\in I
and
y\in V(F)
, if there exists an edge
e\in E(F)
such that
s(e)=x
and
t(e)=y
, then
y\in I
.
The set of upper parts of
F
is denoted by
\mathrm{Up}(F)
.

2.

Let
I\subseteq V(F)
. The forest of planted rooted trees
F_{\mid I}
is defined by

\displaystyle V(F_{\mid I})

\displaystyle=I,

\displaystyle E(F_{\mid I})

\displaystyle=\{e\in E(F)\mid t(e)\in I\}.

This is a forest of planted rooted trees (note that it is necessary to add an undecorated root to each component part).
Moreover, if
F\in S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V}))
, then by restriction of the decorations,
F_{\mid I}\in S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V}))
.

Notations 3.2
.

Any forest of planted rooted trees of
S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E}\otimes D_{V}))
will be denoted under the form

x=F\otimes\underbrace{\bigotimes_{e\in E(F)}d_{e}\otimes\bigotimes_{v\in V(F)}d_{v}}_{=W_{F}},

where
F
is the underlying, non-decorated forest of planted rooted trees. In order to simplify the notations,
we shall denote tensor products
x\otimes x^{\prime}
of elements of
S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E}\otimes D_{V}))^{\otimes 2}
under the form

x\otimes x^{\prime}=F\otimes F^{\prime}\otimes W_{F}\otimes W_{F^{\prime}},

rather than

x\otimes x^{\prime}=(F\otimes W_{F})\otimes(F^{\prime}\otimes W_{F^{\prime}}),

and we shall use similar notations for superior tensor products of
S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E}\otimes D_{V}))
.

Proposition 3.17
.

Let
\phi
be a tree-compatible map. We give the algebra
S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E}\otimes D_{V}))
a coproduct
\Delta^{\phi}
, defined as follows, making it a bialgebra:
for any forest
x=F\otimes W_{F}
of planted decorated rooted trees,

\Delta^{\phi}(x)=\sum_{I\in\mathrm{Up}(F)}F_{\mid I}\otimes F_{\mid V(F)\setminus I}\otimes\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\notin I,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}^{\circ}\phi_{e,s(e)}(W_{F}).

Remark 3.2
.

The tree-compatibility is needed to correctly define this coproduct, as the order in
\displaystyle\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\notin I,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}^{\circ}\phi_{e,s(e)}
does not matter if
\phi
is tree-compatible.

Example 3.8
.

Let
a_{1},a_{2},a_{3}\in D_{E}
and
b_{1},b_{2},b_{3}\in D_{V}
.

\displaystyle\Delta^{\phi}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 7.38889pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\hbox{\vtop{\halign{\entry@#!@&&\entry@@#!@\cr&\cr&\cr&\crcr}}}\ignorespaces{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-7.38889pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{1}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 15.26865pt\raise-15.6111pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a_{1}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern 31.38889pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{2}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 34.65753pt\raise-15.6111pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a_{2}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\kern-3.0pt\raise-31.2222pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.5pt\hbox{$\textstyle{}$}}}}}}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern 31.38889pt\raise-31.2222pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{3}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 34.65753pt\raise-46.35934pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a_{3}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\kern-3.0pt\raise-61.49649pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.5pt\hbox{$\textstyle{}$}}}}}}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern 33.27777pt\raise-61.49649pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.3264pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\star}$}}}}}\ignorespaces}}}}\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle=x\otimes 1+1\otimes x+\sum_{i}\begin{array}[]{c}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 12.54529pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\hbox{\vtop{\halign{\entry@#!@&&\entry@@#!@\cr\cr\crcr}}}\ignorespaces{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-7.38889pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 17.7292pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\hbox{\vtop{\halign{\entry@#!@&&\entry@@#!@\cr\cr\cr\crcr}}}\ignorespaces{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-7.38889pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a_{2}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-17.7292pt\raise-31.2222pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.88043pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\phi_{V}^{i}(b_{3})}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern-4.12024pt\raise-46.35934pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a_{3}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-5.5pt\raise-61.49649pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.3264pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\star}$}}}}}\ignorespaces}}}}\end{array}+\sum_{i}\begin{array}[]{c}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 12.54529pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\hbox{\vtop{\halign{\entry@#!@&&\entry@@#!@\cr\cr\crcr}}}\ignorespaces{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-7.38889pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{2}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern-12.54529pt\raise-15.6111pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.30008pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\phi_{E}^{i}(a_{2})}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-5.5pt\raise-31.2222pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.3264pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\star}$}}}}}\ignorespaces}}}}\end{array}\otimes\begin{array}[]{c}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 17.7292pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\hbox{\vtop{\halign{\entry@#!@&&\entry@@#!@\cr\cr\cr\crcr}}}\ignorespaces{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-7.38889pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{1}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern-4.12024pt\raise-15.6111pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a_{1}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-17.7292pt\raise-31.2222pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.88043pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\phi_{V}^{i}(b_{3})}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern-4.12024pt\raise-46.35934pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a_{3}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-5.5pt\raise-61.49649pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.3264pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\star}$}}}}}\ignorespaces}}}}\end{array}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i,j}\begin{array}[]{c}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 12.54529pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\hbox{\vtop{\halign{\entry@#!@&&\entry@@#!@\cr\cr\crcr}}}\ignorespaces{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-7.38889pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{1}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern-12.54529pt\raise-15.6111pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.30008pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\phi_{E}^{i}(a_{1})}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-5.5pt\raise-31.2222pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.3264pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\star}$}}}}}\ignorespaces}}}}\end{array}\begin{array}[]{c}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 12.54529pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\hbox{\vtop{\halign{\entry@#!@&&\entry@@#!@\cr\cr\crcr}}}\ignorespaces{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-7.38889pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{2}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern-12.54529pt\raise-15.6111pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.30008pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\phi_{E}^{j}(a_{2})}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-5.5pt\raise-31.2222pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.3264pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\star}$}}}}}\ignorespaces}}}}\end{array}\otimes\begin{array}[]{c}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 27.7917pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\hbox{\vtop{\halign{\entry@#!@&&\entry@@#!@\cr\cr\crcr}}}\ignorespaces{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-27.7917pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.88043pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\phi_{V}^{i}\circ\phi_{V}^{j}(b_{3})}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern-4.12024pt\raise-15.6111pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a_{1}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-5.5pt\raise-31.2222pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.3264pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\star}$}}}}}\ignorespaces}}}}\end{array}.

Proof.

Let
x=F\otimes W_{F}\in S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E}\otimes D_{V}))
.

\displaystyle(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\Delta^{\phi})\circ\Delta^{\phi}(x)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}V(F)=I\sqcup J\sqcup K,\\
I\in\mathrm{Up}(F),\\
J\in\mathrm{Up}(F_{\mid J\sqcup K})\end{subarray}}F_{\mid I}\otimes F_{\mid J}\otimes F_{\mid K}\otimes\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\in K,\>t(e)\in J\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pt\phi_{e,s(e)}\circ\hskip-8.53581pt\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\in J\sqcup K,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pt\phi_{e,s(e)}(W_{F}),

\displaystyle(\Delta^{\phi}\otimes\mathrm{Id})\circ\Delta^{\phi}(x)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}V(F)=I\sqcup J\sqcup K,\\
I\sqcup J\in\mathrm{Up}(F),\\
I\in\mathrm{Up}(F_{\mid I\sqcup J})\end{subarray}}F_{\mid I}\otimes F_{\mid J}\otimes F_{\mid K}\otimes\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\in J,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pt\phi_{e,s(e)}\circ\hskip-8.53581pt\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\in K,\>t(e)\in I\sqcup J\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pt\phi_{e,s(e)}(W_{F}).

If
V(F)=I\sqcup J\sqcup K
, then it is not difficult to show that

I\in\mathrm{Up}(F)\mbox{ and }J\in\mathrm{Up}(F_{\mid J\sqcup K})\Longleftrightarrow I\sqcup J\in\mathrm{Up}(F)\mbox{ and }I\in\mathrm{Up}(F_{\mid I\sqcup J}).

The tree-compatibility of
\phi
implies the commutation of the maps
\phi_{e,s(e)}
, so

\displaystyle\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\in K,\>t(e)\in J\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pt\phi_{e,s(e)}\circ\hskip-8.53581pt\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\in J\sqcup K,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pt\phi_{e,s(e)}

\displaystyle=\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\in K,\>t(e)\in J\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pt\phi_{e,s(e)}\circ\hskip-8.53581pt\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\in K,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pt\phi_{e,s(e)}\circ\hskip-8.53581pt\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\in J,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pt\phi_{e,s(e)}

\displaystyle=\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\in J,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pt\phi_{e,s(e)}\circ\hskip-8.53581pt\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\in K,\>t(e)\in I\sqcup J\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pt\phi_{e,s(e)}.

So
\Delta^{\phi}
is coassociative.
If
F,G
are two forests of planted rooted trees, then

\mathrm{Up}(FG)=\{I\sqcup J\mid(I,J)\in\mathrm{Up}(F)\otimes\mathrm{Up}(G)\}.

This gives the multiplicativity of
\Delta^{\phi}
.
∎

Proposition 3.18
.

Let
\phi,\psi
be two tree-compatible maps on
D_{E}\otimes D_{V}
such that

\psi_{13}\circ\phi_{23}=\phi_{23}\circ\psi_{13}.

The map
\overline{\Theta}_{\phi}
is a morphism from the bialgebra
(S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})),m,\Delta^{\psi\circ\phi})
to the bialgebra
(S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})),m,\Delta^{\psi})
. It is an isomorphism if, and only if,
\phi
is bijective.

Proof.

The map
\overline{\Theta}_{\phi}
is obviously an algebra morphism for the product
m
. Let
F\otimes W_{F}\in(S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V}))
.

\displaystyle\Delta^{\psi}\circ\overline{\Theta}_{\phi}(F\otimes W_{F})

\displaystyle=\sum_{I\in\mathrm{Up}(F)}F_{\mid I}\otimes F_{\mid V(F)\setminus I}\otimes\underbrace{\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\notin I,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}\psi_{e,s(e)}\circ\prod^{\circ}_{e\in E(F)}\phi_{e,s(e)}}_{F_{I}}(W_{F}).

By definition of an upper part, if
e\in E(F)
, then if
s(e)\in I
, necessarily
t(e)\in I
. This gives, using the different commutations between the
\phi_{e,s(e)}
and
\psi_{e,s(e)}
,

\displaystyle F_{I}

\displaystyle=\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\notin I,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}\psi_{e,s(e)}\circ\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\notin I,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}\phi_{e,s(e)}\circ\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\in I,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}\phi_{e,s(e)}\circ\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\notin I,\>t(e)\notin I\end{subarray}}\phi_{e,s(e)}

\displaystyle=\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\notin I,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}(\psi\circ\phi)_{e,s(e)}\circ\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\in I,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}\phi_{e,s(e)}\circ\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\notin I,\>t(e)\notin I\end{subarray}}\phi_{e,s(e)}

\displaystyle=\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\in I,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}\phi_{e,s(e)}\circ\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\notin I,\>t(e)\notin I\end{subarray}}\phi_{e,s(e)}\circ\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\notin I,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}(\psi\circ\phi)_{e,s(e)}.

Consequently,

\displaystyle\Delta^{\psi}\circ\overline{\Theta}_{\phi}(F\otimes W_{F})

\displaystyle=\sum_{I\in\mathrm{Up}(F)}F_{\mid I}\otimes F_{\mid V(F)\setminus I}

\displaystyle\otimes\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\in I,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}\phi_{e,s(e)}\circ\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\notin I,\>t(e)\notin I\end{subarray}}\phi_{e,s(e)}\circ\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\notin I,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}(\psi\circ\phi)_{e,s(e)}(W_{F})

\displaystyle=(\overline{\Theta}_{\phi}\otimes\overline{\Theta}_{\phi})\left(\sum_{I\in\mathrm{Up}(F)}F_{\mid I}\otimes F_{\mid V(F)\setminus I}\otimes\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(F),\\
s(e)\notin I,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}(\psi\circ\phi)_{e,s(e)}(W_{F})\right)

\displaystyle=(\overline{\Theta}_{\phi}\otimes\overline{\Theta}_{\phi})\circ\Delta^{\psi\circ\phi}(F\otimes W_{F}).

So
\overline{\Theta}_{\phi}
is a coalgebra morphism.
∎

A particular case is
\phi=\mathrm{Id}_{D_{E}\otimes D_{V}}
. In this case, we simply write
\Delta
instead of
\Delta^{\mathrm{Id}_{D_{E}\otimes D_{V}}}
. We obtain:

Corollary 3.19
.

Let
\phi
be a tree-compatible map
D_{E}\otimes D_{V}
.
The map
\overline{\Theta}_{\phi}
is a bialgebra morphism from
(S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})),m,\Delta^{\phi})
to
(S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V})),m,\Delta)
.
It is an isomorphism if, and only if,
\phi
is bijective.

3.5
Duality

When
D_{E}
and
D_{V}
are finite-dimensional, we can identify
(D_{E}\otimes D_{V})^{*}
and
D_{E}^{*}\otimes D_{V}^{*}
. We can then transpose any map
\phi:D_{E}\otimes D_{V}\longrightarrow D_{E}\otimes D_{V}
into a map

\phi^{*}:D_{E}^{*}\otimes D_{V}^{*}\longrightarrow D_{E}^{*}\otimes D_{V}^{*}
. As
(\phi_{13})^{*}=\phi^{*}_{13}
and
(\phi_{23})^{*}=\phi^{*}_{23}
,
\phi
is tree-compatible if, and only if,
\phi^{*}
is tree-compatible.
If so,
(S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E}\otimes D_{V}),\star^{\phi},\Delta)
is a graded bialgebra, whose graduation is given by the number of vertices. If
D_{E}
and
D_{V}
are finite-dimensional, the components of this graded bialgebra are finite-dimensional.
As a consequence, the graded dual, which we identify with
S(D_{E}^{*}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E}^{*}\otimes D_{V}^{*}))
, is also a graded bialgebra, isomorphic to
(S(D_{E}^{*}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E}^{*}\otimes D_{V}^{*})),m,\Delta^{\phi^{*}})
, as we shall see in Theorem
3.20
.

More generally, we now consider two tree-compatible maps
\phi:D_{E}\otimes D_{V}\longrightarrow D_{E}\otimes D_{V}
and
\phi^{\prime}:D^{\prime}_{E}\otimes D^{\prime}_{V}\longrightarrow D^{\prime}_{E}\otimes D^{\prime}_{V}
,
and a pairing
\langle-,-\rangle:(D^{\prime}_{E}\otimes D^{\prime}_{V})\times(D_{E}\otimes D_{V})\longrightarrow\mathbb{K}
such that for any
a\in D_{E}
,
b\in D_{V}
,
a^{\prime}\in D^{\prime}_{E}
,
b^{\prime}\in D^{\prime}_{V}
,

\langle\phi^{\prime}(a^{\prime}\otimes b^{\prime}),a\otimes b\rangle=\langle a^{\prime}\otimes b^{\prime},\phi(a\otimes b)\rangle.

We define a pairing between
S(D^{\prime}_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D^{\prime}_{E},D^{\prime}_{V}))
and
S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V}))
as follows. If
x=F\otimes W_{F}\in S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E},D_{V}))
and
x^{\prime}=F^{\prime}\otimes W^{\prime}_{F}\in S(D^{\prime}_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D^{\prime}_{E},D^{\prime}_{V}))
,

\langle x^{\prime},x\rangle=\sum_{\sigma\in\mathrm{Iso}(F^{\prime},F)}\prod_{e\in E(F^{\prime})}\underbrace{\langle d^{\prime}_{e}\otimes d^{\prime}_{t(e)},d_{\sigma(e)}\otimes d_{\sigma(t(e))}\rangle}_{\langle W^{\prime}_{F},W_{F}\rangle_{\sigma}}.

Note that in a planted forest
F^{\prime}
,
t
is a bijection from
E(F^{\prime})
to
V(F^{\prime})
; hence, in
\langle W^{\prime}_{F},W_{F}\rangle_{\sigma}
, each
d^{\prime}_{e}
for
e\in E(F^{\prime})
,
d_{e}
for
e\in E(F)
,
d^{\prime}_{v}
for
v\in V(F^{\prime})
,
d_{v}
for
v\in V(F)
, appears exactly once.
If the pairing
\langle-,-\rangle
between
D^{\prime}_{E}\otimes D^{\prime}_{V}
and
D_{E}\otimes D_{V}
is non-degenerate, then this extension is non-degenerate.

Example 3.9
.

Let
a_{1},a_{2},a_{3}\in D_{E}
,
a^{\prime}_{1},a^{\prime}_{2},a^{\prime}_{3}\in D^{\prime}_{E}
,
b_{1},b_{2},b_{3}\in D_{V}
, and
b^{\prime}_{1},b^{\prime}_{2},b^{\prime}_{3}\in D^{\prime}_{V}
.

\displaystyle\langle\begin{array}[]{c}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 7.38889pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\hbox{\vtop{\halign{\entry@#!@&&\entry@@#!@\cr\cr\cr\cr\crcr}}}\ignorespaces{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-7.38889pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-3.00891pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b^{\prime}_{1}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern-4.12024pt\raise-15.6111pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.3978pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a^{\prime}_{1}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-7.38889pt\raise-31.2222pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-3.00891pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b^{\prime}_{2}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern-4.12024pt\raise-46.35934pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.3978pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a^{\prime}_{2}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-7.38889pt\raise-61.49649pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-3.00891pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b^{\prime}_{3}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern-4.12024pt\raise-74.82808pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.3978pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a^{\prime}_{3}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-5.5pt\raise-88.15968pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.3264pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\star}$}}}}}\ignorespaces}}}}\end{array},\begin{array}[]{c}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 7.38889pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\hbox{\vtop{\halign{\entry@#!@&&\entry@@#!@\cr\cr\cr\cr\crcr}}}\ignorespaces{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-7.38889pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{1}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern-4.12024pt\raise-15.6111pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a_{1}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-7.38889pt\raise-31.2222pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{2}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern-4.12024pt\raise-46.35934pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a_{2}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-7.38889pt\raise-61.49649pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{3}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern-4.12024pt\raise-74.82808pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a_{3}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-5.5pt\raise-88.15968pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.3264pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\star}$}}}}}\ignorespaces}}}}\end{array}\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle a^{\prime}_{1}\otimes b^{\prime}_{1},a_{1}\otimes b_{1}\rangle\langle a^{\prime}_{2}\otimes b^{\prime}_{2},a_{2}\otimes b_{2}\rangle\langle a^{\prime}_{3}\otimes b^{\prime}_{3},a_{3}\otimes b_{3}\rangle,

\displaystyle\langle\begin{array}[]{c}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 7.38889pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\hbox{\vtop{\halign{\entry@#!@&&\entry@@#!@\cr&\cr&\cr&\crcr}}}\ignorespaces{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-7.38889pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-3.00891pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b^{\prime}_{1}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 15.26865pt\raise-15.75446pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.3978pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a^{\prime}_{1}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern 31.38889pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-3.00891pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b^{\prime}_{2}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 34.65753pt\raise-15.75446pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.3978pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a^{\prime}_{2}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\kern-3.0pt\raise-31.50891pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.5pt\hbox{$\textstyle{}$}}}}}}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern 31.38889pt\raise-31.50891pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-3.00891pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b^{\prime}_{3}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 34.65753pt\raise-46.71773pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.3978pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a^{\prime}_{3}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\kern-3.0pt\raise-61.92656pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.5pt\hbox{$\textstyle{}$}}}}}}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern 33.27777pt\raise-61.92656pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.3264pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\star}$}}}}}\ignorespaces}}}}\end{array},\begin{array}[]{c}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 7.38889pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\hbox{\vtop{\halign{\entry@#!@&&\entry@@#!@\cr&\cr&\cr&\crcr}}}\ignorespaces{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern-7.38889pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{1}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 15.26865pt\raise-15.6111pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a_{1}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@drawline@}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern 31.38889pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{2}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 34.65753pt\raise-15.6111pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a_{2}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\kern-3.0pt\raise-31.2222pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.5pt\hbox{$\textstyle{}$}}}}}}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern 31.38889pt\raise-31.2222pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.72223pt\hbox{$\textstyle{b_{3}}$}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 34.65753pt\raise-46.35934pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{a_{3}}$}}}}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\kern-3.0pt\raise-61.49649pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.5pt\hbox{$\textstyle{}$}}}}}}}{{{\hbox{\framed@@{0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\kern 33.27777pt\raise-61.49649pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.3264pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\star}$}}}}}\ignorespaces}}}}\end{array}\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle a^{\prime}_{1}\otimes b^{\prime}_{1},a_{1}\otimes b_{1}\rangle\langle a^{\prime}_{2}\otimes b^{\prime}_{2},a_{2}\otimes b_{2}\rangle\langle a^{\prime}_{3}\otimes b^{\prime}_{3},a_{3}\otimes b_{3}\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle a^{\prime}_{1}\otimes b^{\prime}_{1},a_{2}\otimes b_{2}\rangle\langle a^{\prime}_{2}\otimes b^{\prime}_{2},a_{1}\otimes b_{1}\rangle\langle a^{\prime}_{3}\otimes b^{\prime}_{3},a_{3}\otimes b_{3}\rangle.

Theorem 3.20
.

The pairing
\langle-,-\rangle
is a Hopf pairing between
(S(D^{\prime}_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D^{\prime}_{E}\otimes D^{\prime}_{V})),\star^{\phi^{\prime}},\Delta)
and
(S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E}\otimes D_{V})),m,\Delta^{\phi})
, that is to say
for any
x^{\prime},y^{\prime}\in S(D^{\prime}_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D^{\prime}_{E}\otimes D^{\prime}_{V}))
, for any
x,y\in(S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E}\otimes D_{V}))
,

\displaystyle\langle 1,x\rangle

\displaystyle=\varepsilon(x),

\displaystyle\langle x^{\prime},1\rangle

\displaystyle=\varepsilon(x^{\prime}),

\displaystyle\langle x^{\prime}\star^{\phi^{\prime}}y^{\prime},x\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle x^{\prime}\otimes y^{\prime},\Delta^{\phi}(x)\rangle,

\displaystyle\langle\Delta(x^{\prime}),x\otimes y\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle x^{\prime},xy\rangle.

Proof.

Let us consider
x^{\prime}=F^{\prime}\otimes W^{\prime}_{F}
and
y^{\prime}=G^{\prime}\otimes W^{\prime}_{G}
in
S(D^{\prime}_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D^{\prime}_{E}\otimes D^{\prime}_{V}))
and
x=H\otimes W_{H}\in S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E}\otimes D_{V}))
. We consider the two sets

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle=\{(I,\sigma_{F},\sigma_{G})\mid I\in\mathrm{Up}(H),\>\sigma_{F}\in\mathrm{Iso}(F^{\prime},H_{\mid I}),\>\sigma_{G}\in\mathrm{Iso}(G^{\prime},H_{\mid V(H)\setminus I})\},

\displaystyle B

\displaystyle=\{(g,\sigma)\mid g\in\mathbf{G}(F^{\prime},G^{\prime}),\>\sigma\in\mathrm{Iso}(F^{\prime}\curvearrowright_{g}G^{\prime}),H)\}.

If
g\in\mathbf{G}(F^{\prime},G^{\prime})
, then
V(F^{\prime})
is an upper part of
F^{\prime}\curvearrowright_{g}G^{\prime}
. Therefore, we define a map
\Upsilon:A\longrightarrow B
, sending
(g,\sigma)
to
(\sigma(V(F^{\prime})),\sigma_{\mid V(F^{\prime})},\sigma_{\mid V(G^{\prime})})
. Its is clearly injective.
If
(I,\sigma_{F},\sigma_{F})\in A
, let us define
g\in\mathbf{G}(F^{\prime},G^{\prime})
as follows. If
T^{\prime}\in\mathbf{T}(F^{\prime})
, let us denote its undecorated root by
r(T)
. Two cases are possible.

1.

If
\sigma_{F}(r(T))
is a root of
H
, we put
g(T)=0
.

2.

If
\sigma_{F}(r(T))
is not a root of
H
, there exists a unique edge
e\in E(H)
such that
t(e)=\sigma_{F}(r(T))
. As
I
is an upper part of
H
,
s(e)\notin I
. We put
g(T)=\sigma_{G}^{-1}(s(e))
.

Then
\sigma=\sigma_{F}\sqcup\sigma_{G}:V(F^{\prime})\sqcup V(G^{\prime})=V(F^{\prime}\curvearrowright G)^{\prime}\longrightarrow V(H)
is an isomorphism and
\Upsilon(g,\sigma)=(I,\sigma_{F},\sigma_{G})
. So
\Upsilon
is a bijection. We obtain

\displaystyle\langle x^{\prime}\star^{\phi^{\prime}}y^{\prime},x\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathbf{G}(F^{\prime},G^{\prime})}\langle F\curvearrowright_{g}G,\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}T\in\mathbf{T}(F^{\prime}),\\
g(T)\neq 0\end{subarray}}\phi^{\prime}_{e(T),g(T)}(W^{\prime}_{F}\otimes W^{\prime}_{G}),H\otimes W_{H}\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{(g,\sigma)\in B}\langle\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}T\in\mathbf{T}(F^{\prime}),\\
g(T)\neq 0\end{subarray}}\phi^{\prime}_{e(T),g(T)}(W^{\prime}_{F}\otimes W^{\prime}_{G}),W_{H}\rangle_{\sigma}

\displaystyle=\sum_{(g,\sigma)\in B}\langle W^{\prime}_{F}\otimes W^{\prime}_{G},\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}T\in\mathbf{T}(F^{\prime}),\\
g(T)\neq 0\end{subarray}}\phi_{\sigma(e(T)),\sigma(g(T))}(W_{H})\rangle_{\sigma}

\displaystyle=\sum_{(I,\sigma_{F},\sigma_{G})\in A}\langle W^{\prime}_{F}\otimes W^{\prime}_{G},\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(H),\\
s(e)\notin I,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}\phi_{e,s(e)}(W_{H})\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{I\in\mathrm{Up}(H)}\langle F^{\prime}\otimes W^{\prime}_{F}\otimes G^{\prime}\otimes W^{\prime}_{G},H_{\mid H}\otimes H_{\mid V(H)\setminus I}\otimes\prod^{\circ}_{\begin{subarray}{c}e\in E(H),\\
s(e)\notin I,\>t(e)\in I\end{subarray}}\phi_{e,s(e)}(W_{H})\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle x^{\prime}\otimes y^{\prime},\Delta^{\phi}(x)\rangle.

Let
x^{\prime}=F^{\prime}\otimes W^{\prime}_{F}\in S(D^{\prime}_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D^{\prime}_{E}\otimes D^{\prime}_{V}))
and
x=F\otimes W_{F}
,
y=G\otimes W_{G}\in S(D_{E}\otimes\mathcal{T}(D_{E}\otimes D_{V}))
.

\displaystyle\langle x^{\prime},m(x\otimes y)\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle F^{\prime}\otimes W^{\prime}_{F},FG\otimes W_{F}\otimes W_{G}\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{\sigma\in\mathrm{Iso}(F^{\prime},FG)}\langle W^{\prime}_{F},W_{F}W_{G}\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{T}(F^{\prime})=\mathbf{T}_{1}\sqcup\mathbf{T}_{2}}\langle\prod_{T^{\prime}\in\mathbf{T}_{1}}T^{\prime}\otimes W^{\prime}_{T},F\otimes W_{F}\rangle\langle\prod_{T^{\prime}\in\mathbf{T}_{2}}T^{\prime}\otimes W^{\prime}_{T},G\otimes W_{G}\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle\Delta(x^{\prime}),x\otimes y\rangle.

So
\langle-,-\rangle
is a Hopf pairing.
∎

4
Examples

4.1
An example for stochastic PDEs, without noise

In this paragraph,we consider the case described in
[
6
]
, firstly without noise.

Notations 4.1
.

We fix
d\in\mathbb{N}
and put

D_{E}^{\mathrm{s}}=D_{V}^{\mathrm{s}}=\mathrm{Vect}\left(\mathbb{N}^{d+1}\right).

Elements of
\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
will be written under the form
a=(a_{0},\ldots,a_{d})
.
For
j\in\llbracket 0;d\rrbracket
, we denote by
\epsilon^{(j)}
the element of
\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
defined by

\displaystyle\forall i\in\llbracket 0;d\rrbracket,

\displaystyle\epsilon^{(j)}_{i}

\displaystyle=\delta_{i,j}.

Definition 4.1
.

The map
\partial^{(j)}
is defined by

\partial^{(j)}:\left\{\begin{array}[]{rcl}D_{E}^{\mathrm{s}}\otimes D_{V}^{\mathrm{s}}&\longrightarrow&D_{E}^{\mathrm{s}}\otimes D_{V}^{\mathrm{s}}\\
a\otimes b&\longmapsto&b_{j}\left(a-\epsilon^{(j)}\right)\otimes\left(b-\epsilon^{(j)}\right),\end{array}\right.

with the convention that for any
c\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
,
c-\epsilon^{(j)}=0
if
c_{j}=0
.

Lemma 4.2
.

For any
i,j\in\llbracket 0;d\rrbracket
,
\partial^{(i)}\circ\partial^{(j)}=\partial^{(j)}\circ\partial^{(i)}
.

Proof.

Let us assume that
i\neq j
. Let
a,b\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
.

\displaystyle\partial^{(i)}\circ\partial^{(j)}(a\otimes b)

\displaystyle=b_{j}\partial^{(i)}\left(\left(a-\epsilon^{(j)}\right)\otimes\left(b-\epsilon^{(j)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=b_{i}b_{j}\left(a-\epsilon^{(j)}-\epsilon^{(i)}\right)\otimes\left(b-\epsilon^{(j)}-\epsilon^{(i)}\right)=\partial^{(j)}\circ\partial^{(i)}(a\otimes b).\qed

Proposition 4.3
.

For any
\lambda=(\lambda_{0},\ldots,\lambda_{d})\in\mathbb{K}^{d+1}
,
\partial^{\lambda}=\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{d}\lambda_{i}\partial^{(i)}
is tree-compatible.

Proof.

Let us first prove that for any
i,j\in\llbracket 0;d\rrbracket
,

\partial^{(i)}_{13}\circ\partial^{(j)}_{23}=\partial^{(j)}_{23}\circ\partial^{(i)}_{12}.

Let
a,a^{\prime},b\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
.

\displaystyle\partial^{(i)}_{13}\circ\partial^{(j)}_{23}(a\otimes a^{\prime}\otimes b)

\displaystyle=b_{j}\partial_{13}^{(i)}\left(a\otimes(a^{\prime}-\epsilon^{(j)})\otimes(b-\epsilon^{(j)})\right)

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}b_{i}b_{j}\left(a-\epsilon^{(i)}\right)\otimes(a^{\prime}-\epsilon^{(j)})\otimes\left(b-\epsilon^{(j)}-\epsilon^{(i)}\right)\mbox{ if }i\neq j,\\
b_{i}(b_{i}-1)\left(a-\epsilon^{(i)}\right)\otimes\left(a^{\prime}-\epsilon^{(i)}\right)\otimes\left(b-2\epsilon^{(j)}\right)\mbox{ if }i=j;\end{cases}

\displaystyle\partial^{(j)}_{23}\circ\partial^{(i)}_{13}(a\otimes a^{\prime}\otimes b)

\displaystyle=b_{i}\partial_{23}^{(j)}\left((a-\epsilon^{(i)})\otimes a^{\prime}\otimes(b-\epsilon^{(i)})\right)

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}b_{j}b_{i}\left(a-\epsilon^{(i)}\right)\otimes(a^{\prime}-\epsilon^{(j)})\otimes\left(b-\epsilon^{(j)}-\epsilon^{(i)}\right)\mbox{ if }i\neq j,\\
b_{i}(b_{i}-1)\left(a-\epsilon^{(i)}\right)\otimes\left(a^{\prime}-\epsilon^{(i)}\right)\otimes\left(b-2\epsilon^{(j)}\right)\mbox{ if }i=j.\end{cases}

So
\partial^{(i)}_{13}\circ\partial^{(j)}_{23}=\partial^{(j)}_{23}\circ\partial^{(i)}_{13}
. By Proposition
2.3
,
\phi^{\lambda}
is tree-compatible for any
\lambda\in\mathbb{K}^{d+1}
.
∎

Let us compute the maps
(\partial^{\lambda})^{n}
, which are tree-compatible by Proposition
2.4
.

Notations 4.2
.

1.

If
a,b\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
, we shall say that
a\leq b
if for any
i\in\llbracket 0;d\rrbracket
,
a_{i}\leq b_{i}
. If so, we put

\binom{b}{a}=\prod_{i=0}^{d}\binom{b_{i}}{a_{i}}=\prod_{i=0}^{d}\frac{b_{i}!}{a_{i}!(b_{i}-a_{i})!}.

2.

If
a,b\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
, we put
\min(a,b)=(\min(a_{0},b_{0}),\ldots,\min(a_{n},b_{n}))
.

3.

For any
a\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
, we put
|a|=a_{0}+\cdots+a_{d}
.

4.

For any
\lambda=(\lambda_{0},\ldots,\lambda_{d})\in\mathbb{K}^{d+1}
and any
a=(a_{0},\ldots,a_{d})\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
, we put

\lambda^{a}=\prod_{i=0}^{d}\lambda_{i}^{a_{i}},

with the convention
0^{0}=1
.

Lemma 4.4
.

For any
\lambda\in\mathbb{K}^{d+1}
, for any
n\in\mathbb{N}
, and for any
a,b\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
,

\displaystyle(\partial^{\lambda})^{n}(a\otimes b)

\displaystyle=n!\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1},\\
l\leq\min(a,b),\\
|l|=n\end{subarray}}\lambda^{l}\binom{b}{l}(a-l)\otimes(b-l).

Proof.

By induction on
n
. If
n=0
, this is obvious. If
n=1
,

\displaystyle\partial^{\lambda}(a\otimes b)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in\llbracket 0;d\rrbracket,\\
a_{i},b_{i}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\lambda_{i}b_{i}\left(a-\epsilon^{(i)}\right)\otimes\left(b-\epsilon^{(i)}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in\llbracket 0;d\rrbracket,\\
a_{i},b_{i}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\lambda^{\epsilon^{(i)}}\binom{b}{\epsilon^{(i)}}\left(a-\epsilon^{(i)}\right)\otimes\left(b-\epsilon^{(i)}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1},\\
l\leq\min(a,b),\\
|l|=1\end{subarray}}\lambda^{l}\binom{b}{l}(a-l)\otimes(b-l).

So the results holds at rank
1
. Let us assume it at rank
n
.

\displaystyle(\partial^{\lambda})^{n+1}(a\otimes b)

\displaystyle=n!\partial^{\lambda}\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1},\\
l\leq\min(a,b),\\
|l|=n\end{subarray}}\lambda^{l}\binom{b}{l}(a-l)\otimes(b-l)\right)

\displaystyle=n!\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1},\\
l\leq\min(a,b),\\
|l|=n\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in\llbracket 0;d\rrbracket,\\
a_{i}-l_{i},b_{i}-l_{i}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\lambda^{l}\lambda_{i}\binom{b}{l}(b_{i}-l_{i})\left(a-l-\epsilon^{(i)}\right)\otimes\left(b-l-\epsilon^{(i)}\right)

\displaystyle=n!\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1},\\
l\leq\min(a,b),\\
|l|=n+1\end{subarray}}\lambda^{l}\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in\llbracket 0;d\rrbracket,\\
l_{i}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\binom{b}{l-\epsilon^{(i)}}(b_{i}-l_{i}+1)\right)(a-l)\otimes(b-l).

Let
l\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
, such that
l\leq b
. Then

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in\llbracket 0;d\rrbracket,\\
l_{i}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\binom{b}{l-\epsilon^{(i)}}(b_{i}-l_{i}+1)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in\llbracket 0;d\rrbracket,\\
l_{i}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\prod_{j\neq i}\frac{b_{j}!}{l_{j}!(b_{j}-l_{j})!}\frac{b_{i}!}{(l_{i}-1)!(b_{i}-l_{i}+1)!}(b_{i}-l_{i}+1)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in\llbracket 0;d\rrbracket,\\
l_{i}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\prod_{j\neq i}\frac{b_{j}!}{l_{j}!(b_{j}-l_{j})!}\frac{b_{i}!}{l_{i}!(b_{i}-l_{i})!}l_{i}

\displaystyle=\binom{b}{l}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in\llbracket 0;d\rrbracket,\\
l_{i}\geq 1\end{subarray}}l_{i}

\displaystyle=\binom{b}{l}|l|.

We obtain

\displaystyle(\partial^{\lambda})^{n+1}(a\otimes b)

\displaystyle=n!\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1},\\
l\leq\min(a,b),\\
|l|=n+1\end{subarray}}\lambda^{l}(n+1)\binom{b}{l}(a-l)\otimes(b-l)

\displaystyle=(n+1)!\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1},\\
l\leq\min(a,b),\\
|l|=n+1\end{subarray}}\lambda^{l}\binom{b}{l}(a-l)\otimes(b-l).\qed

The maps
\partial^{(i)}
are locally nilpotent: if
a\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
,

\left(\partial^{(i)}\right)^{a_{i}+1}(a)=0.

As they pairwise commute, any linear span of
\partial^{(i)}
is also locally nilpotent, so for any
\lambda\in\mathbb{K}^{d+1}
,
\partial^{\lambda}
is locally nilpotent: we can use Proposition
2.4
with
\phi=\partial^{\lambda}
.

Proposition 4.5
.

For any
\lambda\in\mathbb{K}^{d+1}
, we put

\phi^{\lambda}=\exp\left(\partial^{\lambda}\right)=\exp\left(\lambda_{0}\partial^{(0)}\right)\circ\cdots\circ\exp\left(\lambda_{d}\partial^{(d)}\right).

Then
\phi^{\lambda}
is tree-compatible. Moreover, for any
a,b\in\mathbb{K}^{d+1}
,

\phi^{\lambda}(a\otimes b)=\sum_{l\leq\min(a,b)}\lambda^{l}\binom{b}{l}(a-l)\otimes(b-l).

For any
\lambda,\mu\in\mathbb{K}^{d+1}
,
\phi^{\lambda}\circ\phi^{\mu}=\phi^{\lambda+\mu}
. The map
\phi^{\lambda}
is invertible, of inverse
\phi^{-\lambda}
.

Proof.

By Proposition
2.4
,
\phi^{\lambda}
is tree-compatible for any
\lambda\in\mathbb{K}^{d+1}
. If
\lambda,\mu\in\mathbb{K}^{d+1}
, as the
\partial^{(i)}
pairwise commute,

\displaystyle\phi^{\lambda}\circ\phi^{\mu}

\displaystyle=\exp\left(\lambda_{0}\partial^{(0)}\right)\circ\cdots\circ\exp\left(\lambda_{d}\partial^{(d)}\right)\circ\exp\left(\mu_{0}\partial^{(0)}\right)\circ\cdots\circ\exp\left(\mu_{d}\partial^{(d)}\right)

\displaystyle=\exp\left(\lambda_{0}\partial^{(0)}\right)\circ\exp\left(\mu_{0}\partial^{(0)}\right)\circ\cdots\circ\exp\left(\lambda_{d}\partial^{(d)}\right)\circ\exp\left(\mu_{d}\partial^{(d)}\right)

\displaystyle=\exp\left((\lambda_{0}+\mu_{0})\partial^{(0)}\right)\circ\cdots\circ\exp\left((\lambda_{d}+\mu_{d})\partial^{(d)}\right)

\displaystyle=\phi^{\lambda+\mu}.

As
\phi^{0}=\mathrm{Id}_{D_{E}^{\mathrm{s}}\otimes D_{V}^{\mathrm{s}}}
,
\phi^{\lambda}
is invertible and
(\phi^{\lambda})^{-1}=\phi^{-\lambda}
.
∎

From Theorem
3.3
:

Theorem 4.6
.

The
D_{E}^{\mathrm{s}}
-multiple pre-Lie algebra
(\mathcal{T}(D_{E}^{\mathrm{s}},D_{V}^{\mathrm{s}}),\triangleleft^{\phi^{\lambda}})
is freely generated by the elements
\hbox to5.69pt{\vbox to4.27pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.84523pt\lower-1.42271pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\lxSVG@roundcap\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-linecap} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\lxSVG@roundjoin\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-linejoin} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces

is an isomorphism from

Proposition 4.7
.

We consider the case where
\lambda_{i}=1
for any
i\in\llbracket 0;d\rrbracket
.
Let us consider
P=\mathrm{Vect}(X_{i}\mid i\in\llbracket 0;d\rrbracket)
. We then define
\psi_{V}
and
\psi_{E}
by

\displaystyle\forall i\in\llbracket 0;d\rrbracket,\>\forall a,b\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1},

\displaystyle\psi_{V}(X_{i})(b)

\displaystyle=b+\epsilon^{(i)},

\displaystyle\psi_{E}(X_{i})(a)

\displaystyle=a-\epsilon^{(i)},

with the convention that
a-\epsilon^{(i)}=0
if
a_{i}=0
.
Then
(\psi_{E},\psi_{V})
is
\phi^{\lambda}
-compatible, in the sense of Corollary
3.10
.

Proof.

Only (
11
) is not immediate. Let
a,b\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
and let
i\in\llbracket 0;d\rrbracket
.

\displaystyle\phi\circ(\mathrm{Id}_{D_{E}^{\mathrm{s}}}\otimes\psi_{V}(X_{i}))(a\otimes b)-(\mathrm{Id}_{D_{E}^{\mathrm{s}}}\otimes\psi_{V}(X_{i}))\circ\phi(a\otimes b)

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\leq\min(a,b+\epsilon^{(i)})}\binom{b+\epsilon^{(i)}}{l}(a-l)\otimes(b-l+\epsilon^{(i)})-\sum_{l\leq\min(a,b)}\binom{b}{l}(a-l)\otimes(b-l+\epsilon^{(i)})

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\leq\min(a,b)}\left(\binom{b+\epsilon^{(i)}}{l}-\binom{b}{l}\right)(a-l)\otimes(b-l+\epsilon^{(i)})

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\leq\min(a,b+\epsilon^{(i)}),\\
l_{i}=b_{i}+1\end{subarray}}\binom{b+\epsilon^{(i)}}{l}(a-l)\otimes(b-l+\epsilon^{(i)}).

If
l\leq\min(a,b)
,

\displaystyle\binom{b+\epsilon^{(i)}}{l}-\binom{b}{l}

\displaystyle=\prod_{j\neq i}\binom{b_{j}}{l_{j}}\left(\frac{(b_{i}+1)!}{l_{i}!(b_{i}-l_{i}+1)!}-\frac{b_{i}!}{l_{i}!(b_{i}-l_{i})!}\right)

\displaystyle=\binom{b}{l}\left(\frac{b_{i}+1}{b_{i}-l_{i}+1}-1\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{l_{i}}{b_{i}-l_{i}+1}\binom{b}{l}.

If
l\leq\min(a,b+\epsilon^{(i)})
, with
l_{i}=b_{i}+1
,

\binom{b+\epsilon^{(i)}}{l}=\prod_{j\neq i}\binom{b_{j}}{l_{j}}.

We obtain finally

\displaystyle\phi\circ(\mathrm{Id}_{D_{E}^{\mathrm{s}}}\otimes\psi_{V}(X_{i}))(a\otimes b)-(\mathrm{Id}_{D_{E}^{\mathrm{s}}}\otimes\psi_{V}(X_{i}))\circ\phi(a\otimes b)

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\leq\min(a,b)}\frac{l_{i}}{b_{i}-l_{i}+1}\binom{b}{l}(a-l)\otimes(b-l+\epsilon^{(i)})

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\leq\min(a,b+\epsilon^{(i)}),\\
l_{i}=b_{i}+1\end{subarray}}\prod_{j\neq i}\binom{b_{j}}{l_{j}}(a-l)\otimes(b-l+\epsilon^{(i)})

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\leq\min(a,b),\\
l_{i}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{l_{i}}{b_{i}-l_{i}+1}\binom{b}{l}(a-l)\otimes(b-l+\epsilon^{(i)})

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\leq\min(a,b+\epsilon^{(i)}),\\
l_{i}=b_{i}+1\end{subarray}}\prod_{j\neq i}\binom{b_{j}}{l_{j}}(a-l)\otimes(b-l+\epsilon^{(i)}).

(12)

Let us firstly assume that
a_{i}=0
. Then, in (
12
), both sums are equal to
0
. Moreover,
\psi_{E}(X_{i})(a)=0
, so

\phi\circ(\psi_{E}(X_{i})\otimes\mathrm{Id}_{D_{V}^{\mathrm{s}}})(a\otimes b)=0.

Let us now assume that
a_{i}\geq 1
. Then

\displaystyle\phi\circ(\psi_{E}(X_{i})\otimes\mathrm{Id}_{D_{V}^{\mathrm{s}}})(a\otimes b)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\leq\min(a,b+\epsilon^{(i)}),\\
l_{i}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\binom{b}{l-\epsilon^{(i)}}(a-l)\otimes(b-l+\epsilon^{(i)}).

If
l\leq\min(a,b)
and
l_{i}\geq 1
, then

\displaystyle\binom{b}{l-\epsilon^{(i)}}

\displaystyle=\prod_{j\neq i}\binom{b_{j}}{l_{j}}\frac{b_{i}!}{(l_{i}-1)!(b_{i}-l_{i}+1)!}=\frac{l_{i}}{b_{i}-l_{i}+1}\binom{b}{l}.

If
l\leq\min(a,b)
and
l_{i}=b_{i}+1
, then

\displaystyle\binom{b}{l-\epsilon^{(i)}}

\displaystyle=\prod_{j\neq i}\binom{b_{j}}{l_{j}}.

We obtain

\displaystyle\phi\circ(\psi_{E}(X_{i})\otimes\mathrm{Id}_{D_{V}^{\mathrm{s}}})(a\otimes b)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\leq\min(a,b),\\
l_{i}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{l_{i}}{b_{i}-l_{i}+1}\binom{b}{l}(a-l)\otimes(b-l+\epsilon^{(i)})

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\leq\min(a,b+\epsilon^{(i)}),\\
l_{i}=b_{i}+1\end{subarray}}\prod_{j\neq i}\binom{b_{j}}{l_{j}}(a-l)\otimes(b-l+\epsilon^{(i)}).

(13)

Comparing (
12
) and (
13
), we obtain the result.
∎

Therefore, we obtain a post-Lie algebra, as mentioned in
[
6
]
.

.

and
